1: // /Users/saudceo/flick-backend/src/middleware/authMiddleware.js
2: const jwt = require("jsonwebtoken");
3: const User = require("../models/user");
4: const Session = require("../models/session");
5: 
6: const protect = async (req, res, next) => {
7:   const authorization =
8:     typeof req.headers.authorization === "string"
9:       ? req.headers.authorization.trim()
10:       : "";
11: 
12:   if (
13:     !authorization ||
14:     !authorization.toLowerCase().startsWith("bearer ")
15:   ) {
16:     return res.status(401).json({
17:       error: "Not authorized, no token",
18:     });
19:   }
20: 
21:   const token = authorization.slice(7).trim();
22: 
23:   if (!token) {
24:     return res.status(401).json({
25:       error: "Not authorized, no token",
26:     });
27:   }
28: 
29:   try {
30:     const decoded = jwt.verify(
31:       token,
32:       process.env.JWT_SECRET,
33:       {
34:         algorithms: ["HS256"],
35:       }
36:     );
37: 
38:     if (!decoded?.id) {
39:       return res.status(401).json({
40:         error: "Token is invalid or expired",
41:       });
...
88:       error.message
89:     );
90: 
91:     return res.status(401).json({
92:       error: "Token is invalid or expired",
93:     });
94:   }
95: };
96: 
97: module.exports = protect;

===== src/middleware/protectOptional.js =====
...
1: const jwt = require("jsonwebtoken");
2: const User = require("../models/user");
3: 
4: const protectOptional = async (req, res, next) => {
5:   const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
6:   if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ")) return next();
7: 
8:   try {
9:     const token = authHeader.split(" ")[1];
10:     const decoded = jwt.verify(token, process.env.JWT_SECRET);
11:     req.user = await User.findById(decoded.id).select("_id");
12:   } catch {
13:     req.user = null;
14:   }
15: 
16:   next();
17: };
18: 
19: module.exports = protectOptional;

858:   try {
859:     if (
860:       this.isModified("username") &&
861:       typeof this.username === "string"
862:     ) {

865:         .replace(/\s+/g, "");
866:     }
867: 
868:     if (
869:       this.isModified("email") &&
870:       typeof this.email === "string"
871:     ) {
872:       this.email = this.email
873:         .trim()
874:         .toLowerCase();
875:     }
876: 
877:     if (typeof this.name === "string") {
878:       this.name = this.name.trim();
879:     }
880: 
881:     if (typeof this.bio === "string") {
882:       this.bio = this.bio.trim();
883:     }
884: 
885:     if (typeof this.avatar === "string") {
886:       this.avatar = this.avatar.trim();
887:     }
888: 
889:     if (typeof this.profileBackdrop === "string") {
890:       this.profileBackdrop =
891:         this.profileBackdrop.trim();
892:     }

895:       this.tvProfileBackdrop =
896:         this.tvProfileBackdrop.trim();
897:     }

899:     if (this.isModified("password") && this.password) {
900:       const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
901:       this.password = await bcrypt.hash(
902:         this.password,
903:         salt
904:       );
905:     }
906: 
907:     // ==================================================
908:     // Movie watchlist
909:     // ==================================================

34:   {
35:     userId: 1,
36:     movieId: 1,
37:   },
38:   {
39:     unique: true,
40:   }
41: );
42: 
43: // Useful when loading all custom movie posters for one user.
44: customPosterSchema.index({
45:   userId: 1,
46:   updatedAt: -1,
47: });
48: 
49: customPosterSchema.pre("validate", function (next) {
50:   try {
51:     if (typeof this.posterUrl === "string") {
52:       this.posterUrl = this.posterUrl.trim();
53:     }
54: 
55:     next();
56:   } catch (error) {
57:     next(error);
58:   }
59: });
60: 
61: module.exports =
62:   mongoose.models.CustomPoster ||
63:   mongoose.model("CustomPoster", customPosterSchema);

505: // GET /api/lists/friends
506: router.get(
507:   "/friends",
508:   protect,
509:   async (req, res) => {
510:     try {
511:       const user = await User.findById(
512:         req.user._id
513:       )
514:         .select("following")
515:         .lean();
516: 
517:       if (!user) {
518:         return res.status(404).json({
519:           message: "User not found",
520:         });
521:       }
522: 
523:       const followingIds =
524:         Array.isArray(user.following)
525:           ? user.following
526:           : [];
527: 
528:       if (!followingIds.length) {
529:         return res.json([]);
530:       }
531: 
532:       const {
533:         limit,
534:         skip,
535:       } = parsePagination(
536:         req.query,
537:         50,
538:         100
539:       );
540: 
541:       const lists = await List.find({
542:         user: {
543:           $in: followingIds,
544:         },
545:         isPrivate: false,
546:       })
547:         .sort({
548:           updatedAt: -1,
549:           _id: -1,
550:         })
551:         .skip(skip)
552:         .limit(limit)
553:         .populate(
554:           "user",
555:           "username avatar"
556:         )
557:         .lean();
558:

564:       );

735:   "/user/:userId",
736:   protectOptional,
737:   async (req, res) => {
738:     try {
739:       const { userId } = req.params;
740: 
741:       if (!isValidObjectId(userId)) {
742:         return res.status(400).json({
743:           message: "Invalid user ID",
744:         });
745:       }
746: 
747:       const userExists = await User.exists({
748:         _id: userId,
749:       });
750: 
751:       if (!userExists) {
752:         return res.status(404).json({
753:           message: "User not found",
754:         });
755:       }
756: 
757:       const isOwner =
758:         req.user &&
759:         String(req.user._id) ===
760:           String(userId);
761: 
762:       const filter = isOwner
763:         ? {
764:             user: userId,
765:           }
766:         : {
767:             user: userId,
768:             isPrivate: false,
769:           };
770: 
771:       const {
772:         limit,
773:         skip,
774:       } = parsePagination(
775:         req.query,
776:         100,
777:         100
778:       );
779: 
780:       const lists = await List.find(filter)
781:         .sort({
782:           updatedAt: -1,
783:           _id: -1,
784:         })
785:         .skip(skip)
786:         .limit(limit)
787:         .populate(
788:           "user",
789:           "username avatar"
790:         )
791:         .lean();
792: 
793:       return res.json(lists);
794:     } catch (error) {
795:       console.error(
796:         "❌ Failed to fetch user lists:",
797:         error
798:       );
799:

801:         message:
802:           "Failed to get user lists",
803:       });
804:     }
805:   }

1533:               ).trim()

132: // 🔍 Search users by username
133: router.get('/search', async (req, res) => {
134:   const query =
135:   typeof req.query.query === "string"
136:     ? req.query.query.trim().slice(0, 50)
137:     : "";
138: 
139:   if (!query.trim()) return res.json([]);
140: 
141:   try {
142:     const users = await User.find({
143:       username: {
144:         $regex: escapeRegex(query),
145:         $options: "i",
146:       },
147:     })
148:       .select("username avatar _id")
149:       .limit(20);
150: 
151:     // 🧠 Prioritize exact matches first
152:     users.sort((a, b) => {
153:       const aExact = a.username.toLowerCase() === query.toLowerCase();
154:       const bExact = b.username.toLowerCase() === query.toLowerCase();
155: 
156:       if (aExact && !bExact) return -1;
157:       if (!aExact && bExact) return 1;
158:       return a.username.localeCompare(b.username);
159:     });
160: 
161:     res.json(users);
162:   } catch (err) {
163:     console.error("❌ User search error:", err);
164:     res.status(500).json({ message: "Search failed", error: err.message });
165:   }
166: });

202: router.post("/:userId/follow/:targetId",protect,async (req, res) => {
203:     try {
204:       const { userId, targetId } = req.params;
205: 
206:       if (
207:         !isValidObjectId(userId) ||

209:       ) {
210:         return res.status(400).json({
211:           error: "Invalid user ID",
212:         });
213:       }
214: 
215:       if (!requireOwner(req, res, userId)) {
216:         return;
217:       }
218: 
219:       if (String(userId) === String(targetId)) {
220:         return res.status(400).json({
221:           error: "You cannot follow yourself",
222:         });
223:       }
224: 
225:       const [user, targetUser] = await Promise.all([
226:         User.findById(userId),
227:         User.findById(targetId),
228:       ]);
229: 
230:       if (!user || !targetUser) {
231:         return res.status(404).json({
232:           error: "User not found",
233:         });
234:       }
235: 
236:       const isFollowing = user.following.some(
237:         (id) => String(id) === String(targetId)
238:       );
239: 
240:       if (
241:         !isFollowing &&
242:         targetUser.noNewFollowers
243:       ) {
244:         return res.status(403).json({
245:           error: "This user is not accepting new followers",
246:         });
247:       }
248: 
249:       if (isFollowing) {
250:         await Promise.all([
251:           User.updateOne(
252:             { _id: userId },
253:             {
254:               $pull: {
255:                 following: targetId,
256:               },
257:             }
258:           ),
259: 
260:           User.updateOne(
261:             { _id: targetId },
262:             {
263:               $pull: {
264:                 followers: userId,
265:               },
266:             }
267:           ),
268:         ]);
269: 
270:         return res.status(200).json({
271:           following: false,
272:           message: "Unfollowed user",
273:         });
274:       }

277:         User.updateOne(
...
318:

2256: // ✅ Fast Watchlist (no TMDB calls)
2257: router.get("/:userId/watchlist", async (req, res) => {
2258:   const { userId } = req.params;
2259:   const rawSort = req.query.sort || "added";
2260:   const rawOrder = req.query.order === "asc" ? "asc" : "desc";
2261:   const genre = req.query.genre ? Number(req.query.genre) : null;
2262: 
2263:   const ALLOWED_SORTS = new Set(["added", "release", "rating", "runtime"]);
2264:   const sort = ALLOWED_SORTS.has(rawSort) ? rawSort : "added";
2265:   const dir = rawOrder === "asc" ? 1 : -1;
2266: 
2267:   const toTs = (d) => (d ? new Date(d).getTime() || 0 : 0);
2268: 
2269:   try {
2270:     const user = await User.findById(userId).lean();
2271:     if (!user || !user.watchlist) {
2272:       return res.status(404).json({ error: "User or watchlist not found" });
2273:     }
2274: 
2275:     let movies = user.watchlist.filter(
2276:       (w) => typeof w === "object" && w.tmdbId
2277:     );
2278: 
2279:     // Hydrate any entries missing runtime, rating, release_date, or genres
2280:     const needHydrate = movies.filter(
2281:       (m) =>
2282:         !Array.isArray(m.genres) ||
2283:         m.genres.length === 0 ||
2284:         m.runtime == null ||
2285:         m.vote_average == null ||
2286:         !m.release_date
2287:     );
2288: 
2289:     if (needHydrate.length) {
2290:       await Promise.all(
2291:         needHydrate.map(async (m) => {
2292:           try {
2293:             const d = await getMovieDetails(m.tmdbId);
2294:             m.genres = d?.genres || [];
2295:             m.runtime = d?.runtime ?? null;
2296:             m.vote_average = d?.vote_average ?? null;
2297:             m.release_date = d?.release_date ?? null;
2298: 
2299:             await User.updateOne(
2300:               { _id: userId, "watchlist.tmdbId": m.tmdbId },
2301:               {
2302:                 $set: {
2303:                   "watchlist.$.genres": m.genres,
2304:                   "watchlist.$.runtime": m.runtime,
2305:                   "watchlist.$.vote_average": m.vote_average,
2306:                   "watchlist.$.release_date": m.release_date,
2307:                 },
2308:               }
2309:             );
2310:           } catch (e) {
2311:             // ignore per-item errors
2312:           }
2313:         })
2314:       );
2315:     }
2316: 
2317:     // Apply genre filter if requested
2318:     if (Number.isFinite(genre) && genre > 0) {
2319:       movies = movies.filter(
2320:         (m) =>
2321:           Array.isArray(m.genres) && m.genres.some((g) => g.id === genre)
2322:       );
2323:     }
2324: 
2325:     // Safe sort
2326:     movies.sort((a, b) => {
2327:       if (sort === "added") {
2328:         return (toTs(a.addedAt) - toTs(b.addedAt)) * dir;
2329:       }
2330:       if (sort === "release") {
2331:         return (toTs(a.release_date) - toTs(b.release_date)) * dir;
2332:       }
2333:       if (sort === "rating") {
2334:         const av = Number.isFinite(a?.vote_average) ? a.vote_average : 0;
2335:         const bv = Number.isFinite(b?.vote_average) ? b.vote_average : 0;
2336:         return (av - bv) * dir;
2337:       }
2338:       if (sort === "runtime") {
2339:         const ar = Number.isFinite(a?.runtime) ? a.runtime : 0;
2340:         const br = Number.isFinite(b?.runtime) ? b.runtime : 0;
2341:         return (ar - br) * dir;
2342:       }
2343:       return ((a.title || "").localeCompare(b.title || "")) * dir;
2344:     });
2345: 
2346:     // Custom posters
2347:     const customPosters = await CustomPoster.find({
2348:       userId,
2349:       movieId: { $in: movies.map((m) => String(m.tmdbId)) },
2350:     }).lean();
2351: 
2352:     const postersMap = {};
2353:     for (const cp of customPosters) {
2354:       postersMap[String(cp.movieId)] = cp.posterUrl;
2355:     }
2356: 
2357:     // Enrich missing poster_path if needed
2358:     const enriched = await Promise.all(
2359:       movies.map(async (m) => {
2360:         let poster_path = m.poster_path || null;
2361:         let title = m.title || null;
2362:         let release_date = m.release_date || null;
2363: 
2364:         if (!poster_path) {
2365:           try {
2366:             const d = await getMovieDetails(m.tmdbId);
2367:             poster_path = d?.poster_path || null;
2368:             title = title || d?.title || null;
2369:             release_date = release_date || d?.release_date || null;
2370:           } catch {
2371:             // ignore per-item fetch errors
2372:           }
2373:         }
2374: 
2375:         return {
2376:           ...m,
2377:           posterOverride: postersMap[String(m.tmdbId)] || null,
2378:           poster_path,
2379:           title,
2380:           release_date,
2381:         };
2382:       })
2383:     );
2384: 
2385:     res.json(enriched);
2386:   } catch (err) {
2387:     console.error("❌ Failed to fetch watchlist", err);
2388:     res.status(500).json({ error: "Could not fetch watchlist" });
2389:   }
2390: });

110: router.post("/register", async (req, res) => {
111:   try {
112:     let {
113:       name,
114:       username,
115:       email,
116:       password,
117:       avatar,
118:     } = req.body || {};
119: 
120:     name =
121:       typeof name === "string"
122:         ? name.trim()
123:         : "";
124: 
125:     username =
126:       typeof username === "string"
127:         ? username.trim()
128:         : "";
129: 
130:     email =
131:       typeof email === "string"
132:         ? email.trim().toLowerCase()
133:         : "";
134: 
135:     if (!username || !email || !password) {
136:       return res.status(400).json({
137:         error: "Missing required fields",
138:       });
139:     }
140: 
141:     // Email deliverability check — warning only for now.
142:     try {
143:       const check =
144:         await validateEmailDeliverability(email);
145: 
146:       if (!check.ok) {
147:         console.warn(
148:           "⚠️ Email deliverability warning:",
149:           email,
150:           check.reason
151:         );
152:       }
153:     } catch (err) {
154:       console.warn(
155:         "⚠️ Email deliverability check failed:",
156:         err.message
157:       );
158:     }
159: 
160:     /*
161:      * Check email first.
162:      *
163:      * This allows an unverified account to go through the
164:      * resend-verification flow instead of immediately returning
165:      * "Username already taken" for its own username.
166:      */
167:     const existingUser = await User.findOne({
168:       email,
169:     }).select("+password");
170: 
171:     if (existingUser) {
172:       /*
173:        * Only accounts explicitly marked false enter this flow.
174:        * This prevents older accounts with an undefined value
175:        * from accidentally being treated as unverified.
176:        */
177:       if (existingUser.emailVerified === false) {
178:         let passwordMatches = false;
179: 
180:         if (
181:           typeof existingUser.matchPassword ===
182:           "function"
183:         ) {
184:           passwordMatches =
185:             await existingUser.matchPassword(
186:               password
187:             );
188:         }
189: 
190:         /*
191:          * Never return an authenticated token merely because
192:          * someone knows the email address of an unverified user.
193:          */
194:         if (!passwordMatches) {
195:           return res.status(409).json({
196:             error:
197:               "Account already exists. Please log in or reset your password.",
198:           });
199:         }
200: 
...
252:           refreshToken:
253:             authSession.refreshToken,
254: 
255:           refreshTokenExpiresAt:
256:             authSession.refreshTokenExpiresAt,
257: 
258:           user: {
259:             _id: existingUser._id,
260:             name: existingUser.name,
261:             username:
262:               existingUser.username,
263:             email: existingUser.email,
264:             avatar: existingUser.avatar,
265:             emailVerified: false,
266:           },
267:         });
268:       }
269: 
270:       return res.status(409).json({
271:         error: "Email already in use",
272:       });
273:     }

277:      * regular expression before checking the username.

755: // 🔐 Login
756: router.post('/login', async (req, res, next) => {
757:   try {
758:     const { email, username, password } = req.body || {};
759:     if (!password || (!email && !username)) {
760:       return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Email/Username and password are required' });
761:     }
762: 
763:     const query = email
764:       ? { email: String(email).toLowerCase().trim() }
765:       : { username: String(username).trim() };
766: 
767:     // Select password explicitly (schema often has select:false)
768:     const user = await User.findOne(query).select('+password');
769:     if (!user) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'User not found' });
770: 
771:     // Some Google-only accounts have no real hash
772:     if (!user.password || user.password === 'google-oauth') {
773:       return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Account requires Google login' });
774:     }
775: 
776:     // Use your model method if present; otherwise compare safely
777:     let isMatch = false;
778:     if (typeof user.matchPassword === 'function') {
779:       isMatch = await user.matchPassword(password);
780:     } else {
781:       const bcrypt = require('bcryptjs');
782:       isMatch = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password);
783:     }
784: 
785:     if (!isMatch) return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid credentials' });
786: 
787:     const authSession = await createAuthSession(
788:       user._id,
789:       req
790:     );
791: 
792:     return res.status(200).json({
793:       message: "Login successful",
794:     
795:       // Existing app still receives the same field.
796:       token: authSession.token,
797:     
798:       // The updated app will store this securely.
799:       refreshToken: authSession.refreshToken,
800:       refreshTokenExpiresAt:
801:         authSession.refreshTokenExpiresAt,
802:     
803:       user: {
804:         _id: user._id,
805:         username: user.username,
806:         email: user.email,
807:         avatar: user.avatar,
808:       },
809:     });
810:   } catch (error) {
811:     return next(error);
812:   }
813: });

1015: // ✏️ Update Profile
1016: router.put('/profile', protect, async (req, res) => {
1017:   const { username, email, password, bio, avatar } = req.body;
1018: 
1019:   try {
1020:     const user = await User.findById(req.user._id);
1021:     if (!user) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'User not found' });
1022: 
1023:     // Username
1024:     if (username) {
1025:       const uname = username.toLowerCase().trim();
1026:       if (uname !== user.username) {
1027:         const exists = await User.findOne({
1028:           _id: { $ne: user._id },
1029:           username: { $regex: `^${uname}$`, $options: 'i' }
1030:         });
1031:         if (exists) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Username already taken' });
1032:         user.username = uname;
1033:       }
1034:     }
1035: 
1036:     // Email (validate deliverability + uniqueness, then re-verify)
1037:     if (email) {
1038:       const newEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();
1039:       if (newEmail !== user.email) {
1040:         const check = await validateEmailDeliverability(newEmail);
1041:         if (!check.ok) {
1042:           return res.status(400).json({
1043:             error: 'Please use a real, deliverable email address',
1044:             reason: check.reason,
1045:           });
1046:         }
1047: 
1048:         const emailTaken = await User.findOne({ _id: { $ne: user._id }, email: newEmail });
1049:         if (emailTaken) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Email already in use' });
1050: 
1051:         user.email = newEmail;
1052:         user.emailVerified = false;
1053: 
1054:         const verificationCode = crypto.randomInt(100000, 999999).toString();
1055:         user.verificationCode = verificationCode;
1056:         user.verificationCodeExpires = new Date(Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000);
1057: 
1058:         await sendEmail(
1059:           newEmail,
1060:           "Verify your new email for Scene",
1061:           `Here’s your code: ${verificationCode}\n\nIt expires in 10 minutes.`
1062:         );
1063:       }
1064:     }
1065: 
1066:     if (password) user.password = password; // hashed in pre-save
1067:     if (bio !== undefined) user.bio = bio;
1068:     if (avatar) user.avatar = avatar;
1069: 
1070:     await user.save();
1071: 
1072:     res.status(200).json({
1073:       message: 'Profile updated successfully',
1074:       user: {
1075:         id: user._id,
1076:         username: user.username,
1077:         email: user.email,
1078:         bio: user.bio,
1079:         avatar: user.avatar,
1080:         emailVerified: !!user.emailVerified,
1081:       },
1082:     });
1083:   } catch (error) {
1084:     console.error('❌ Update profile error:', error);
1085:     res.status(500).json({ error: error.message });
1086:   }
1087: });

1094: [POSSIBLE SECRET ASSIGNMENT REDACTED]

2352: // PATCH /api/tv-logs/:logId
2353: //
2354: // Edit rating, review, media, date, character, or backdrop.
2355: // ======================================================
2356: 
2357: router.patch(
2358:   "/:logId",
2359:   protect,
2360:   async (req, res) => {
2361:     try {
2362:       const userId =
2363:         getAuthenticatedUserId(req);
2364: 
2365:       const allowedFields = [
2366:         "rating",
2367:         "review",
2368:         "containsSpoilers",
2369:         "watchedAt",
2370:         "customEpisodeBackdrop",
2371:         "customShowPoster",
2372:         "gif",
2373:         "image",
2374:         "images",
2375:         "favoriteCharacter",
2376:       ];
2377: 
2378:       const updates = {};
2379: 
2380:       for (
2381:         const field of
2382:           allowedFields
2383:       ) {
2384:         if (
2385:           Object.prototype
2386:             .hasOwnProperty

1: const express = require("express");
2: const mongoose = require("mongoose");
3: 
4: const router = express.Router();
5: 
6: const User = require("../models/user");
7: const protect = require("../middleware/authMiddleware");
8: const {
9:   getMovieDetails,
10: } = require("../services/tmdbService");
11: const CustomPoster = require("../models/customPoster");
12: 
13: const MAX_HYDRATIONS_PER_REQUEST = 30;
14: 
15: // ============================================================
16: // HELPERS
17: // ============================================================
18: 
19: const isValidObjectId = (value) =>
20:   mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(value);
21: 
22: const parseMovieId = (value) => {
23:   const movieId = Number(value);
24: 
25:   if (
26:     !Number.isInteger(movieId) ||
27:     movieId <= 0
28:   ) {
29:     return null;
30:   }
31: 
32:   return movieId;
33: };
34: 
35: const normalizeWatchlist = (watchlist) => {
36:   if (!Array.isArray(watchlist)) {
37:     return [];
38:   }
39: 
40:   const seen = new Set();
41:   const cleaned = [];
42: 
43:   for (const rawItem of watchlist) {
44:     if (
45:       !rawItem ||
46:       typeof rawItem !== "object"
47:     ) {
48:       continue;
49:     }
50: 
51:     const item =
52:       typeof rawItem.toObject === "function"
53:         ? rawItem.toObject()
54:         : rawItem;
55: 
56:     const tmdbId = parseMovieId(
57:       item.tmdbId
58:     );
59: 
60:     if (!tmdbId || seen.has(tmdbId)) {
61:       continue;
62:     }
63: 
64:     seen.add(tmdbId);
65: 
66:     cleaned.push({
67:       ...item,
68:       tmdbId,
69:     });
70:   }
71: 
72:   return cleaned;
73: };
74: 
75: const getGenreId = (genre) => {
76:   if (
77:     genre &&
78:     typeof genre === "object"
...
149:   movieIds
150: ) {
151:   if (
152:     !isValidObjectId(userId) ||
153:     !movieIds.length
154:   ) {
155:     return new Map();
156:   }
157: 
158:   const customPosters =
159:     await CustomPoster.find({
160:       userId,
161: 
162:       movieId: {
163:         $in: movieIds,
164:       },
165:     })
166:       .select("movieId posterUrl")
167:       .lean();
168: 
169:   return new Map(
170:     customPosters.map((poster) => [
171:       Number(poster.movieId),
172:       poster.posterUrl,
173:     ])
174:   );
175: }
176: 
177: // SCENE_WATCHLIST_CLEANUP_20261027
178: // Preview/cleanup are always scoped to the signed-in user's MOVIE watchlist.
179: const Log = require("../models/log");
180: 
181: async function findLoggedWatchlistIds(userId) {
182:   const user = await User.findById(userId).select("watchlist").lean();
183:   if (!user) return null;
184: 
185:   const movieIds = [...new Set(
186:     normalizeWatchlist(user.watchlist).map((m) => m.tmdbId)
187:   )];
188:   if (!movieIds.length) return [];
189: 
190:   const loggedIds = await Log.distinct("tmdbId", {
191:     user: userId,
192:     tmdbId: { $in: movieIds },
193:   });
194:   return loggedIds.map(Number).filter((id) => Number.isInteger(id) && id > 0);
195: }
196: 
197: // GET /api/watchlist/cleanup-preview — read-only; returns a count, never personal logs.
198: router.get("/cleanup-preview", protect, async (req, res) => {
199:   try {
200:     // SCENE_ONE_TIME_CLEANUP_V2
201:     const user = await User.findById(req.user._id)
202:       .select("watchlistCleanupCompletedAt").lean();
203:     if (!user) return res.status(404).json({ message: "User not found" });
204:     if (user.watchlistCleanupCompletedAt) {
205:       return res.json({ count: 0, completed: true });
206:     }
207:     const ids = await findLoggedWatchlistIds(req.user._id);
208:     if (ids === null) return res.status(404).json({ message: "User not found" });
209:     return res.json({ count: ids.length, completed: false });
210:   } catch (error) {
211:     console.error("❌ Watchlist cleanup preview failed:", error?.message || error);
212:     return res.status(500).json({ message: "Could not preview watchlist cleanup" });
213:   }
214: });
215: 
216: // POST /api/watchlist/cleanup-watched — run only after client confirmation.
217: router.post("/cleanup-watched", protect, async (req, res) => {
218:   try {
219:     // SCENE_ONE_TIME_CLEANUP_V2: never offer another cleanup after completion.
220:     const user = await User.findById(req.user._id)
221:       .select("watchlistCleanupCompletedAt").lean();
222:     if (!user) return res.status(404).json({ message: "User not found" });
223:     if (user.watchlistCleanupCompletedAt) {
224:       return res.json({ removedCount: 0, completed: true });
225:     }
226:     const ids = await findLoggedWatchlistIds(req.user._id);
227:     if (ids === null) return res.status(404).json({ message: "User not found" });
228:     if (!ids.length) {
229:       await User.updateOne(
230:         { _id: req.user._id, watchlistCleanupCompletedAt: null },
231:         { $set: { watchlistCleanupCompletedAt: new Date() } }
232:       );
233:       return res.json({ removedCount: 0, completed: true });
234:     }
235: 
236:     const result = await User.updateOne(
237:       { _id: req.user._id },
238:       { $pull: { watchlist: { tmdbId: { $in: ids } } } }
239:     );
240:     // Count is from a fresh query to avoid claiming every candidate was removed
241:     // when another request has modified the watchlist concurrently.
242:     const after = await User.findById(req.user._id).select("watchlist").lean();
243:     const remaining = new Set(normalizeWatchlist(after?.watchlist).map((m) => m.tmdbId));
244:     const removedCount = ids.filter((id) => !remaining.has(id)).length;
245:     // Mark complete only once the removal operation has succeeded.
246:     await User.updateOne(
247:       { _id: req.user._id, watchlistCleanupCompletedAt: null },
248:       { $set: { watchlistCleanupCompletedAt: new Date() } }
249:     );
250:     return res.json({ removedCount, matchedCount: ids.length, completed: true });
251:   } catch (error) {
252:     console.error("❌ Watchlist cleanup failed:", error?.message || error);
253:     return res.status(500).json({ message: "Could not clean watchlist" });
254:   }
255: });
256:

322:   protect,
323:   async (req, res) => {
324:     try {
325:       const movieId = parseMovieId(
326:         req.body.movieId
327:       );
328: 
329:       if (!movieId) {
330:         return res.status(400).json({
331:           error: "Invalid movie ID",
332:         });
333:       }
334: 
335:       const user = await User.findById(
336:         req.user._id
337:       );
338: 
339:       if (!user) {
340:         return res.status(404).json({
341:           error: "User not found",
342:         });
343:       }
344: 
345:       const cleanedWatchlist =
346:         normalizeWatchlist(
347:           user.watchlist
348:         );
349: 
350:       const existingIndex =
351:         cleanedWatchlist.findIndex(
352:           (item) =>
353:             Number(item.tmdbId) ===
354:             movieId
355:         );
356: 
357:       if (existingIndex !== -1) {
358:         cleanedWatchlist.splice(
359:           existingIndex,
360:           1
361:         );
362: 
363:         user.watchlist =
364:           cleanedWatchlist;
365: 
366:         await user.save();
367: 
368:         return res.json({
369:           message:
370:             "Removed from watchlist",
371: 
372:           inWatchlist: false,
373: 
374:           watchlistCount:
375:             cleanedWatchlist.length,
376:         });
377:       }
378: 
379:       let details;
380: 
381:       try {
382:         details =
383:           await getMovieDetails(
384:             movieId
385:           );
386:       } catch (tmdbError) {
387:         console.error(
388:           `❌ Failed to fetch movie ${movieId}:`,
389:           tmdbError.message
390:         );
391: 
392:         return res.status(502).json({
393:           error:
394:             "Failed to fetch movie details",
395:         });
396:       }
397: 
398:       if (!details) {
399:         return res.status(404).json({
400:           error: "Movie not found",
401:         });
402:       }
403: 
404:       cleanedWatchlist.push(
405:         cleanMovieDetails(
406:           movieId,
407:           details
408:         )
409:       );
410: 
411:       user.watchlist =
412:         cleanedWatchlist;
413: 
414:       await user.save();
415: 
416:       return res.json({
417:         message: "Added to watchlist",
418: 
419:         inWatchlist: true,
420: 
421:         watchlistCount:
422:           cleanedWatchlist.length,
423:       });
424:     } catch (error) {

427:         error
428:       );
429: 
430:       return res.status(500).json({
431:         error:
432:           "Failed to toggle watchlist",

434:     }
435:   }

447: // POST /api/watchlist/:userId/watchlist
448: router.post(
449:   "/:userId/watchlist",
450:   protect,
451:   async (req, res) => {
452:     try {
453:       const { userId } = req.params;
454: 
455:       if (!isValidObjectId(userId)) {
456:         return res.status(400).json({
457:           error: "Invalid user ID",

459:       }
460: 
461:       if (
462:         String(userId) !==
463:         String(req.user._id)
464:       ) {

466:           error:
467:             "You can only update your own watchlist",
468:         });
469:       }
470:

472:         req.body.tmdbId
473:       );
474: 
475:       if (!movieId) {
476:         return res.status(400).json({
477:           error: "Invalid tmdbId",
478:         });
479:       }

481:       const user = await User.findById(
482:         userId
483:       );
484: 
485:       if (!user) {
486:         return res.status(404).json({

488:         });

492:         normalizeWatchlist(
493:           user.watchlist

497:         watchlist.some(
498:           (item) =>
499:             Number(item.tmdbId) ===
500:             movieId
501:         );
502: 
503:       if (alreadyExists) {
504:         return res.json({
505:           added: false,
506:           inWatchlist: true,
507:           watchlistCount:
508:             watchlist.length,
509:         });
510:       }
511: 
512:       let details;
513: 
514:       try {
515:         details =
516:           await getMovieDetails(
517:             movieId
518:           );
519:       } catch (tmdbError) {
520:         console.error(
521:           `❌ Failed to fetch movie ${movieId}:`,
522:           tmdbError.message
523:         );
524: 
525:         return res.status(502).json({
526:           error:
527:             "Failed to fetch movie details",
528:         });
529:       }
530: 
531:       if (!details) {
532:         return res.status(404).json({
533:           error: "Movie not found",
534:         });

538:         cleanMovieDetails(
539:           movieId,
540:           details

565:   }

836: router.post(
837:   "/watchlist",
838:   protect,
839:   upload.single("file"),
840:   async (req, res) => {
841:     const sessionId =
842:       createImportSessionId(
843:         "watchlist",
844:         req.user?._id
845:       );
846: 
847:     const startedAt = Date.now();
848: 
849:     try {
850:       logImportEvent(
851:         sessionId,
852:         "IMPORT_SESSION_START",
853:         {
854:           type:
855:             "watchlist",
856: 
857:           originalFileName:
858:             req.file?.originalname ||
859:             "",
860: 
861:           sizeBytes:
862:             Number(
863:               req.file?.size || 0
864:             ),
865:         }
866:       );
867: 
868:       const parsed = parseCsvFile(
869:         req.file
870:       );
871: 
872:       if (parsed.error) {
873:         return res.status(400).json({
874:           message: parsed.error,
875:         });
876:       }
877: 
878:       const user = await User.findById(
879:         req.user._id
880:       );
881: 
882:       if (!user) {
883:         return res.status(404).json({
884:           message: "User not found",
885:         });
886:       }
887: 
888:       const watchlist =
889:         normalizeWatchlist(
890:           user.watchlist
891:         );
892: 
893:       const existingMovieIds = new Set(
894:         watchlist.map((item) =>
895:           Number(item.tmdbId)
896:         )
897:       );
898: 
899:       const matches =
900:         await resolveMovieMatches(
901:           parsed.rows
902:         );
903: 
904:       const movieUpserts = [];
905:       let added = 0;
906:       let duplicates = 0;
907: 
908:       for (const match of matches) {
909:         const movieId = Number(
910:           match.movieData.id
911:         );
912: 
913:         if (
914:           existingMovieIds.has(movieId)
915:         ) {
916:           duplicates += 1;
917:           continue;
918:         }
919: 
920:         existingMovieIds.add(movieId);
921: 
922:         watchlist.push({
923:           tmdbId: movieId,
924: 
925:           title:
926:             match.movieData.title ||
927:             match.title,
928: 
929:           poster_path:
930:             match.movieData
931:               .poster_path ||
932:             null,
933: 
934:           release_date:
935:             match.movieData
936:               .release_date ||
937:             null,
938: 
939:           addedAt: new Date(),
940:         });
941: 
942:         movieUpserts.push(
943:           createMovieUpsert(
944:             match.movieData
945:           )
946:         );
947: 
948:         added += 1;
949:       }
950: 
951:       user.watchlist = watchlist;
952: 
953:       await user.save();
954: 
955:       if (movieUpserts.length) {
956:         try {
957:           await Movie.bulkWrite(
958:             movieUpserts,
959:             {
960:               ordered: false,
961:             }
962:           );
963:         } catch (bulkError) {
964:           console.warn(
965:             "⚠️ Movie cache upsert partially failed:",
966:             bulkError.message
967:           );
968:         }
969:       }
970: 
971:       return res.json({
972:         message:
973:           `✅ Added ${added} movies to your watchlist!`,
974: 
975:         added,
976:         duplicates,
977:         matched: matches.length,
978:         processed: parsed.rows.length,
979:         totalWatchlist:
980:           watchlist.length,
981: 
982:         truncated:
983:           Boolean(parsed.truncated),

988:         error

2: const Log = require("../models/log");
3: const User = require("./models/user");     // adjust path
4: const app = express();
5: 
6: app.get("/review/:id", async (req, res) => {
7:   try {
8:     const review = await Review.findById(req.params.id).populate("user");
9:     if (!review) return res.status(404).send("Review not found");
10: 
11:     const username = review.user?.username || "Scene User";
12:     const stars = "★".repeat(Math.round(review.rating || 0));
13:     const description = review.review || "Check out this review on Scene!";
14:     const backdrop =
15:       review.backdropUrl ||
16:       "https://scenesa.com/default-backdrop.jpg";
17: 
18:     const fullUrl = `https://scenesa.com/review/${review._id}`;
19: 
20:     const html = `
21:       <!doctype html>
22:       <html lang="en">
23:         <head>
24:           <meta charset="utf-8" />
25:           <title>${username} ${stars} – Scene</title>
26: 
27:           <!-- ✅ Open Graph -->
28:           <meta property="og:title" content="${username} ${stars} – Scene" />
29:           <meta property="og:description" content="${description}" />
30:           <meta property="og:image" content="${backdrop}" />
31:           <meta property="og:url" content="${fullUrl}" />
32:           <meta property="og:type" content="article" />
33: 
34:           <!-- ✅ Twitter -->
35:           <meta name="twitter:card" content="summary_large_image" />
36:           <meta name="twitter:title" content="${username} ${stars} – Scene" />
37:           <meta name="twitter:description" content="${description}" />
38:           <meta name="twitter:image" content="${backdrop}" />
39: 
40:           <!-- ✅ Fallback -->
41:           <meta name="theme-color" content="#000000" />
42:         </head>
43:         <body>
44:           <div id="root"></div>
45:           <script src="/main.js"></script>
46:         </body>
47:       </html>
48:     `;
49: 
50:     res.send(html);
51:   } catch (err) {
52:     console.error(err);
53:     res.status(500).send("Server error");
54:   }
55: });

975:             "_id username"

978:

982:             "User not found",

987:         await ShowFavoriteCharacter.find({
988:           user: user._id,

991:             updatedAt: -1,
992:           })
993:           .limit(limit)
994:           .populate(
995:             "user",
996:             "username name avatar"
997:           )
998:           .lean({
999:             virtuals: true,
1000:           });
1001: 
1002:       return res.status(200).json({
1003:         username:
1004:           user.username,
1005: 
1006:         results:
1007:           selections.map(
1008:             serializeSelection
1009:           ),
1010:

1012:           selections.length,
1013:       });

504: // GET /api/tv-watchlist
505: //
506: // Signed-in user's TV watchlist.
507: // ======================================================
508: 
509: router.get(
510:   "/",
511:   protect,
512:   async (req, res) => {
513:     try {
514:       const userId =
515:         getAuthenticatedUserId(req);
516: 
517:       const page =
518:         parsePage(
519:           req.query.page
520:         );
521: 
522:       const limit =
523:         parseLimit(
524:           req.query.limit
525:         );
526: 
527:       const user =
528:         await User.findById(
529:           userId
530:         )
531:           .select(
532:             "tvWatchlist"
533:           )
534:           .lean();
535: 
536:       if (!user) {
537:         return res.status(404).json({
538:           error:
539:             "User not found",
540:         });
541:       }
542: 
543:       const watchlist =
544:         Array.isArray(
545:           user.tvWatchlist
546:         )
547:           ? [...user.tvWatchlist]
548:           : [];
549: 
550:       watchlist.sort(
551:         (first, second) =>
552:           new Date(
553:             second.addedAt || 0
554:           ).getTime() -
555:           new Date(
556:             first.addedAt || 0
557:           ).getTime()
558:       );
559: 
560:       const start =
561:         (page - 1) *
562:         limit;
563: 
564:       const visibleItems =
565:         watchlist.slice(
566:           start,
567:           start + limit
568:         );
569: 
570:       return res.status(200).json({
571:         results:
572:           visibleItems.map(
573:             serializeWatchlistItem
574:           ),
575: 
576:         pagination: {
577:           page,
578:           limit,
579: 
580:           total:
581:             watchlist.length,
582: 
583:           totalPages:
584:             Math.ceil(
585:               watchlist.length /
586:                 limit
587:             ),
588: 
589:           hasMore:
590:             start + limit <
591:             watchlist.length,
592:         },
593:       });
594:     } catch (error) {
595:       return handleError(
596:         error,
597:         res,
598:         "Failed to fetch TV watchlist"
599:       );
600:     }
601:   }
602: );

663: // ======================================================
664: // POST /api/tv-watchlist
665: //
666: // Adds one show.
667: //
668: // Body:
669: // {
670: //   "showTmdbId": 1396
671: // }
672: //
673: // Optional snapshot fields are accepted, but local Show metadata
674: // is preferred when available.
675: // ======================================================
676: 
677: router.post(
678:   "/",
679:   protect,
680:   async (req, res) => {
681:     try {
682:       const userId =
683:         getAuthenticatedUserId(req);
684: 
685:       const showTmdbId =
686:         parsePositiveInteger(
687:           req.body?.showTmdbId ??
688:           req.body?.tmdbId,
689:           "show ID"
690:         );
691: 
692:       const [
693:         user,
694:         localShow,
695:       ] = await Promise.all([
696:         User.findById(userId),
697: 
698:         Show.findOne({
699:           tmdbId:
700:             showTmdbId,
701:         }).lean(),
702:       ]);
703: 
704:       if (!user) {
705:         return res.status(404).json({
706:           error:
707:             "User not found",
708:         });
709:       }
710: 
711:       if (
712:         !localShow &&
713:         !normalizeString(
714:           req.body?.name,
715:           500
716:         )
717:       ) {
718:         return res.status(404).json({
719:           error:
720:             "Show metadata not found. Open the show page before adding it to the watchlist.",
721:         });
722:       }
723: 
724:       if (
725:         !Array.isArray(
726:           user.tvWatchlist
727:         )
728:       ) {
729:         user.tvWatchlist = [];
730:       }
731: 
732:       const existingIndex =
733:         user.tvWatchlist.findIndex(
734:           (item) =>
735:             Number(item.tmdbId) ===
736:             showTmdbId
737:         );
738: 
739:       if (existingIndex >= 0) {
740:         return res.status(200).json({
741:           message:
742:             "Show is already in TV watchlist",
743: 
744:           added: false,
745: 
746:           item:
747:             serializeWatchlistItem(
748:               user.tvWatchlist[
749:                 existingIndex
750:               ]
751:             ),
752:         });
753:       }
754: 
755:       const snapshot =
756:         buildWatchlistSnapshot({
757:           tmdbId:
758:             showTmdbId,
759: 
760:           localShow,
761: 
762:           input:
763:             req.body || {},
764:         });
765: 
766:       user.tvWatchlist.push(
767:         snapshot
768:       );
769: 
770:       await user.save({ validateModifiedOnly: true });
771: 
772:       const addedItem =
773:         user.tvWatchlist.find(
774:           (item) =>
775:             Number(item.tmdbId) ===
776:             showTmdbId
777:         );
778: 
779:       return res.status(201).json({
780:         message:
781:           "Show added to TV watchlist",
782: 
783:         added: true,
784: 
785:         item:
786:           serializeWatchlistItem(
787:             addedItem
788:           ),
789: 
790:         total:
791:           user.tvWatchlist.length,
792:       });
793:     } catch (error) {
794:       return handleError(
795:         error,
796:         res,
797:         "Failed to add show to TV watchlist"
798:       );
799:     }
800:   }
801: );

1: const express = require("express");
2: const router = express.Router();
3: const CustomPoster = require("../models/customPoster"); // ✅ use your existing model
4: const protect = require("../middleware/authMiddleware"); // 🔐 to get req.user
5: 
6: // PATCH /api/posters/:movieId → update poster for a movie
7: router.patch("/:movieId", protect, async (req, res) => {
8:   const { posterUrl } = req.body;
9:   const movieId = Number(req.params.movieId); // 🔢 force number
10:   const userId = req.user._id; // ✅ use the logged-in user
11: 
12:   if (!posterUrl) {
13:     return res.status(400).json({ message: "Poster URL required" });
14:   }
15: 
16:   try {
17:     const updated = await CustomPoster.findOneAndUpdate(
18:       { movieId, userId }, // 🔹 key change: include userId
19:       {
20:         posterUrl,
21:         updatedBy: userId,
22:         updatedAt: new Date(),
23:       },
24:       { upsert: true, new: true }
25:     );
26: 
27:     res.json({ message: "✅ Poster updated", poster: updated });
28:   } catch (err) {
29:     console.error("❌ Failed to update custom poster:", err);
30:     res.status(500).json({ message: "Server error" });
31:   }
32: });
33:

35: module.exports = router;

859: function normalizeWatchlist(value) {
860:   if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
861:     return [];
862:   }
863: 
864:   return value
865:     .filter(Boolean)
866:     .slice()
867:     .sort(
868:       (first, second) =>
869:         new Date(second?.addedAt || 0).getTime() -
870:         new Date(first?.addedAt || 0).getTime(),
871:     );
872: }
873: 
874: function handleError(error, res, fallbackMessage) {
875:   console.error(`❌ ${fallbackMessage}:`, error?.stack || error);
876: 
877:   return res.status(500).json({
878:     error: fallbackMessage,
879: 
880:     details:
881:       process.env.NODE_ENV === "production"
882:         ? undefined
883:         : error?.message || undefined,
884:   });
885: }

1337: // SCENE_TV_TIMELINE_HISTORY_112
1338: // Public profile activity, matching the existing public TV profile visibility.
1339: // Month details are paginated; annual totals are calculated independently.
1340: router.get('/:username/timeline', async (req, res) => {
1341:   try {
1342:     const user = await findUserByUsername(req.params.username);
1343:     if (!user) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'User not found' });
1344: 
1345:     const year = Number(req.query.year);
1346:     const month = Number(req.query.month);
1347:     const page = Math.max(1, Math.min(100000, Number(req.query.page) || 1));
1348:     const limit = 100;
1349:     const offset = Number(req.query.offsetMinutes ?? 0);
1350:     if (!Number.isInteger(year) || year < 1900 || year > 2200 ||
1351:         !Number.isInteger(month) || month < 1 || month > 12 ||
1352:         !Number.isInteger(offset) || Math.abs(offset) > 840) {
1353:       return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid timeline date' });
1354:     }
1355: 
1356:     // JavaScript getTimezoneOffset() is UTC minus local time.
1357:     // Apply that offset to each local calendar boundary.
1358:     const boundary = (y, m) => new Date(Date.UTC(y, m, 1) + offset * 60000);
1359:     const yearStart = boundary(year, 0);
1360:     const yearEnd = boundary(year + 1, 0);
1361:     const monthStart = boundary(year, month - 1);
1362:     const monthEnd = boundary(year, month);
1363:     const dateExpr = { $ifNull: ['$watchedAt', '$createdAt'] };
1364: 
1365:     const [annual, monthLogs] = await Promise.all([
1366:       TVLog.aggregate([
1367:         { $match: { user: user._id } },
1368:         { $addFields: { timelineDate: dateExpr } },
1369:         { $match: { timelineDate: { $gte: yearStart, $lt: yearEnd } } },
1370:         { $bucket: {
1371:           groupBy: '$timelineDate',
1372:           boundaries: Array.from({ length: 13 }, (_, i) => boundary(year, i)),
1373:           output: {
1374:             count: { $sum: 1 },
1375:             ratingSum: { $sum: {
1376:               $cond: [
1377:                 { $gt: [{ $ifNull: ['$rating', 0] }, 0] },
...
1411:       );
1412:       return {
1413:         index,
1414:         count: hit?.count || 0,
1415:         average: hit?.rated ? hit.ratingSum / hit.rated : null
1416:       };
1417:     });
1418:     const hasMore = monthLogs.length > limit;
1419:     return res.json({
1420:       username: user.username,
1421:       year, month, months,
1422:       results: hasMore ? monthLogs.slice(0, limit) : monthLogs,
1423:       hasMore,
1424:       nextPage: hasMore ? page + 1 : null
1425:     });
1426:   } catch (error) {
1427:     return handleError(error, res, 'Failed to fetch TV timeline');
1428:   }
1429: });
